Not for ordinary step-by-step explanations, routine planning or debugging, neutral assumption analysis (use first-principles), or scope trimming (use yagni). Triggers when: planning multi-phase implementations, debugging with uncertain root cause, exploring design alternatives, breaking down ambiguous requirements, or any task where the full scope isn't clear upfront. Teaches revision, branching, and dynamic thought extension (needsMoreThoughts) to avoid linear-only usage patterns. DO NOT USE FOR: ASP.NET Core MVC response JSON (out of scope); distributed cache entry serialization (distributed-caching-and-locking); encrypting serialized values (encrypt-strings). - **series-01-premise**: שלב 01 — הרעיון המרכזי של הסדרה: ההוק, השאלה הגדולה, הטון, ומה הסדרה **אינה**. הפעל על: "יש לי רעיון לסדרה", "מה הסיפור", "הוק לסדרה", "לוגליין", "על מה הסדרה", "מה הטון", series premise, logline, hook, what is the show. זהו השלב הראשון — בלעדיו כל שאר השלבים בונים על חול. ([arielaizn/series-forge](https://github.com/arielaizn/series-forge/tree/HEAD/plugins/series-forge/skills/series-01-premise))

([arielaizn/series-forge](https://github.com/arielaizn/series-forge/tree/HEAD/plugins/series-forge/skills/series-02-research))
